# refactor-2026 Progress Log

Historical entries are retained as evidence. New entries use `[type] YYYY-MM-DD scope: result` with short `start`,
`decision`, `done`, `blocked`, or `next` types. Keep entries concise and current; put detailed analysis and verification
output in the plan or review report. Historical content may be normalized for formatting and repetition, but meaningful
decisions, actions, blockers, and verification results must remain.

Append new entries only at the end of this file; never insert at the head or in the middle. Read the end of the file for the latest state.

[decision] 2026-04-18 YooAsset serialization fact-check: YooAsset uses custom binary stream (ManifestTools.SerializeToBinary), NOT MessagePack. FileSign+FileVersion header, count-prefixed entries. JSON via JsonUtility for debug only
[decision] 2026-04-18 Binary codec technical route: zero-dependency custom binary + editor code generator. [BinarySerializable]/[BinaryField(n)] attributes on data classes, editor tool scans and generates Serializer classes. Eliminates "change field = change two places" maintenance cost while keeping zero external dependencies
[decision] 2026-04-18 Rejected alternatives: (1) MessagePack — developer prefers zero dependency. (2) MemoryPack — Unity 2022.3 Source Generator compatibility risk. (3) Pure handwritten binary (YooAsset-style) — high maintenance cost during manifest evolution phase
[decision] 2026-04-18 Binary header design: lightweight 8-byte fixed header (Magic uint32 + SchemaVersion uint16 + Flags uint16). Per-type independent Magic values (ABManifest='ABMF', etc). Codec determined by file extension (.json/.bin), header for format self-identification and version validation
[decision] 2026-04-18 Schema evolution rules: new fields append at end with incrementing BinaryField number; deleted fields marked [Obsolete] keeping number slot; SchemaVersion increments for field set changes; incompatible changes require major version jump + migration tool
[decision] 2026-04-18 Auto format detection: SerializationUtility.Deserialize<T>(byte[]) checks first 4 bytes against registered Magic → binary codec; no match → JSON fallback. Enables new client reading old JSON files seamlessly
[decision] 2026-04-18 Old backend artifacts (version_state.json, BuildIndex.json) not binary-ized — natural retirement with legacy backend. Only need version mismatch detection to block incompatible reads
[decision] 2026-04-18 BuildIndex.json correction: enters runtime via StreamingAssets (not editor-only), but still not binary-ized as legacy backend artifact
[decision] 2026-04-18 Serialization 4-phase roadmap: S1 (interface+JsonCodec) → S2 (BinaryCodec+code generator) → S3 (ABManifest binary) → S4 (runtime integration+compat layer)
[decision] 2026-04-18 S1 file placement: Utility/Serialization/ as general-purpose infrastructure (same level as HashGenerator/PathManager)
[decision] 2026-04-18 S1 replacement strategy: all 10 JsonUtility call sites replaced in one batch (ABManifest.cs, ManifestLoader.cs, HotfixManager.cs, BuildProjectManager.cs, LocalStatusExporter.cs)
[decision] 2026-04-18 S1 interface design confirmed: ISerializationCodec (byte[]<->T only), JsonCodec (wraps JsonUtility), SerializationUtility (facade: codec registry + format detection + sync/async file I/O), BinaryHeader (Magic registry + HasValidMagic, inactive in S1)
[decision] 2026-04-18 Developer noted HotfixManager should be refactored to new/old backend split (like AssetPackageManager) — separate plan, can be done before or after S1

[decision] 2026-09-10 T3: 新增依赖闭合裁剪 BundleBuildCacheReusePolicy——Unity 只把显式列入本次构建的资产写入内容，未被显式分配的依赖资产会被复制进引用它的每个内容（Unity 2022.3 手册 Asset Duplication），因此被重建内容依赖到的复用内容必须一起重建，否则重建产物与全量构建不一致；闭包在图缺失时保守关闭全部复用。缓存更新只写 `_pending`，由 BuildProjectRunner 交付事务在 Promote 成功后 Commit，失败/未提升 Discard（Task 内不提交）。

[decision] 2026-09-10 T7: 发布事实与退化口径——(1) 目录型目标（本地目录镜像）执行完整发布事务，非目录型目标（Cloudflare 等）无法读取服务器 PackageIndex 与 Manifest，按“完整上传 + 最后上传 PackageIndex”退化，并在回执中标记 DegradedToFullUpload；(2) 已在服务器命中的内容 Hash（含名称不同）允许复用，复用后逐文件校验摘要，复用文件不计入 UploadedCount；(3) “新目录校验未过不得写 PackageIndex”实现为 校验→就位→写索引 的顺序，写索引自带备份与逆序补偿，因此中断既不破坏旧索引也不留半成品包目录；(4) 同名包内容不同且被当前 PackageIndex 指向时拒绝覆盖（已发布内容不可变），内容一致时按幂等成功处理；(5) 非目录型目标不做旧包清理（清理只在目录型目标的独立维护入口）。

[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-ab-handle-boundary：确认 HandleKind 仅保留 Asset/Scene 两种内部释放协议；RawFile 只返回字节，不创建 Handle、不进入缓存且无需显式释放。

[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-ab-scene-lifecycle：确认以唯一 SceneHandle.ReleaseAsync 替代 Release/UnloadAsync；非最后 owner 仅释放 token，最后 owner 异步卸载 Scene 后结算记录与 Bundle；卸载失败保留所有状态供重试。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-ab-scene-lifecycle：确认删除 activateOnLoad、PreloadAsync 及两个固定帧预算；场景加载完成激活后才返回，Single 替换必须等待旧场景真实卸载，不能按帧数结算旧记录或 Bundle。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-ab-editor-boundary：确认 EditorPackageBackend 改名 EditorAssetLoader，仅在 UNITY_EDITOR 的 Editor PlayMode 初始化中创建；复用解析后资源加载契约和 SceneRecord/Handle 语义，不形成 Player、Bundle、热更或公开 Backend 边界。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-hotfix-metadata-simplification：确认删除 IHotfixPipeline.HasRequiredMetadata 与 PersistRemoteMetadataAsync 的 refreshRequiredMetadata 参数；Shared 只要求后端完成目标元数据持久化，AB 始终写 Manifest，AA 在内部决定复用或下载 catalog。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-ab-manifest-boundary：确认 ManifestAssetEntry 仅导出公共运行时资源，保留 Address、资产类型索引、Labels、SourcePath、ContentIndex；删除 EntryId、IsPublic、逐资产 ContentType 与 RuntimeAssetEntry，运行时索引直接使用 ManifestAssetEntry；ManifestContentEntry 保留全部 Content 的物理文件事实与实际依赖。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-runtime-type-index：确认 PrimaryType 统一改名 AssetType，作为 AA/AB 共同的精确资产类型查询键；AA/AB 均在 Editor 构建期以 AssetDatabase.GetMainAssetTypeAtPath 获取实际主类型，AA 停止以首个 Addressables Label 充当类型；统一标识为程序集简单名:类型 FullName，不序列化 System.Type，也不引入 Object/基类可赋值查询。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-manifest-asset-path：确认 SourcePath 改名 AssetPath 并保留为内部加载元数据，业务只使用 Address；ContentIndex 仅定位物理 Content，AssetPath 用于定位其中的 Unity 对象并支持 Editor 直读。Player RawFile 只按 ContentIndex 读取物理文件。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-manifest-content-index：确认保留 ContentIndex 作为 AssetEntry 到 ContentEntries 的 Manifest 内部整数引用；仅在同一原子生成的 Manifest 内有效，不作为跨版本、跨包或业务身份；初始化校验 AssetEntry.ContentIndex 与 DependencyIndices 的范围。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-summary-reuse-minimum：确认删除 CompleteBuildSummary.CollectionFingerprint 及其序列化字段；Summary 复用按单个 Content 执行，只保留构建配方指纹和每个内容的输入指纹等实际复用事实，不保留无消费者的集合级冗余状态。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-content-dependency-helper：确认删除 ContentDependencyIndexResolver；BuildABContentTask 私有承担复用依赖闭包和历史依赖合并，GenerateABManifestTask 私有承担文件名到 DependencyIndices 的转换；依赖事实来源不变。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-content-build-result-name：确认 BundleBuildInfo 改名 ContentBuildResult，BundleName 改名 ContentName，OutputFileName 改名 FileName；该类型只表达单个物理 Content 的构建输出事实，不替代 Task 或整体 BuildResult。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-content-build-item：确认 ContentPlan 改名 ContentBuildItem，定位为 BuildABContentTask 内部单个 Content 的短生命周期可变工作记录；ContentBuildResult 表达任务结束后的物理输出事实。ContentBuildItem 仅保留单一 FileName，删除多输出表达。

[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-content-reuse-state：确认 ContentBuildItem 仅以 ReusedOutput 表达复用状态，以唯一 DependencyFileNames 保存 Summary 回放或 Unity 实际直接依赖；删除 CacheCandidate 与 CachedDependencyFileNames，取消复用时删除 attempt 制品并清空这两个保留事实。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-hotfix-baseline-resolver：确认 HotfixBaselineResolver 是 Shared Editor 只读 Full 基准定位器，按 BackendKey、Platform、Channel 验证并返回 packageRoot 与 fullBuildId；不返回 Summary DTO、不做差异、不写入状态。删除 BuildProjectRunner.HasFullBaseline 粗校验，AA/AB Task 通过该唯一精确入口取得基准。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-version-number-boundary：确认 VersionNumber 仅保留 Major、Minor、Patch、Channel 四项发布版本事实；删除 Build、DailyBuildCount、每日序号计算及 Summary/Index 读写链。构建审计使用 BuildId 与时间戳，版本规划、包身份路径和事实提交分别归 BuildVersionPlanner、BuildRequest、BuildProjectRunner。
[decision] 2026-09-12 fyasset-ab-export-boundary：确认 ExportABOutputTask 只物化已验证的交付 Content、写完整目标 Manifest 与包内 BuildIndex，并创建 Summary 候选；Full/Standalone 按完整 Content 校验大小，Hotfix 按实际交付 Content 校验大小；不承担构建、复验、事实提交、提升、发布、启动数据切换或回滚。
